How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Ancient Golden Dragon Skin
Categories: Ultimate items, Pet Skins, Fire Sales, Pages with UIs
[Ancient Golden Dragon Skin]
Type: Pet Skin ([[Pet Skins]])
Rarity: ULTIMATE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_SKIN_GOLDEN_DRAGON_ANCIENT
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Golden Dragon pets.; Features!; ▶ This skin is swappable between 12; variants, unlocked from Golden Dragon Pet Levels!; ▶ Displays your cosmetic Pet Level above your pet!; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; ULTIMATE COSMETIC
The Ancient Golden Dragon Skin is an [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Ancient Golden Dragon Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 10, 2024. Being [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]], it cost 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 15,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Ancient Golden Dragon Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Golden Dragon Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 12 different animated versions.
- Features!; ▶ This skin is swappable between 12 variants, unlocked from Golden Dragon Pet Levels!; ▶ Displays your cosmetic Pet Level above your pet!

The Awakened Wither Helmet Skin is an [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from the Hypixel Store.
## Obtaining
The Awakened Wither Helmet Skin could be purchased from the Hypixel Store during a 2025 Black Friday promotion.
## Usage
The Awakened Wither Helmet Skin can be applied to a [[Wither Armor|Wither Helmet]], [[Necron's Armor|Necron's Helmet]], [[Storm's Armor|Storm's Helmet]], [[Maxor's Armor|Maxor's Helmet]] or [[Goldor's Armor|Goldor's Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 22 different animated versions.
- Features!; ▶ This skin is swappable between 22 awoke variants.; ▶ Keeps track of the total Dungeon Chests ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]) opened while wearing this skin.

Item ID: PET_SKIN_BLACK_CAT_CATGIRL
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Black Cat pets.; This skin will allow you to swap between multiple variants of your helmet!; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; ULTIMATE COSMETIC
The Catgirl Black Cat Skin is an [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Catgirl Black Cat Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on April 5, 2025. Being [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]], it cost 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 25,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Catgirl Black Cat Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Black Cat Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 96 different animated versions.
- Features!; ▶ This skin is swappable between 96 animated meowable variants.; ▶ Keeps track of the total RARE DROPS! found while wearing this skin.

Apply them just like an enchant book using the anvil.; This skin can be applied to any tier of Crimson Helmet, Aurora Helmet, Terror Helmet, Fervor Helmet, and Hollow Helmet.; Features!; ▶ This skin is swappable between 20; chaotic variants.; ▶ Keeps track of the total Kuudra Chests opened while wearing this skin.; ▶ Adds a particle trail to your bonus Terror Arrows depending on your selected skin.; ULTIMATE COSMETIC
The Chaos Terror Helmet Skin is an [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Chaos Terror Helmet Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on March 14, 2026. Being [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]], it cost 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 10,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Chaos Terror Helmet Skin can be applied to a [[Crimson Armor|Crimson Helmet]], [[Aurora Armor|Aurora Helmet]], [[Terror Armor|Terror Helmet]], [[Fervor Armor|Fervor Helmet]] or [[Hollow Armor|Hollow Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 20 different animated versions.
- Features!; ▶ This skin is swappable between 20 chaotic variants!; ▶ Keeps track of the total Kuudra Chests ([[Kuudra]]) opened while wearing this skin.; ▶ Adds a particle trail to your bonus Terror Arrows ([[Terror Armor]]) depending on your currently selected skin!

The Grand Royal Skin could be purchased from the Hypixel Store during a 2026 Hypixel Store Halloween promotion.

The Great Shark Magma Lord Skin is an [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Great Shark Magma Lord Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on August 10, 2024. Being [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]], it cost 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 17,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Great Shark Magma Lord Skin can be applied to a [[Magma Lord Armor|Magma Lord Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 14 different animated versions.
- Features!; ▶ This skin is swappable between 14 extraordinary colorful sharks!; ▶ Modifies the look of fished up [[Fire Eel]] and [[Taurus]] Sea Creatures based on the currently selected skin!

The Gummy Worm Scatha Skin is an [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Gummy Worm Scatha Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on November 22, 2025. Being [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]], it cost 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 6,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Gummy Worm Scatha Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Scatha Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 15 different animated versions.
- Features!; ▶ This skin is swappable between 15 gummy worms!; ▶ Modifies the look of your spawned Worms ([[Stoneworm]]) and Scathas ([[Scatha]])!

The Harvester Helmet Skin is an [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Harvester Helmet Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on April 6, 2024. Being [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]], it cost 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 15,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Harvester Helmet Skin can be applied to a [[Fermento Armor|Fermento Helmet]] or [[Helianthus Armor|Helianthus Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. The Petal Rose Dragon Skin is an [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that can be purchased from the Hypixel Store.
## Obtaining
The Petal Rose Dragon Skin can be purchased from the Hypixel Store as part of the 2026 Summer SkyBlock Bundle.
## Usage
The Petal Rose Dragon Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Rose Dragon Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 14 different animated versions.

The Sentinel Warden Skin is an [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Sentinel Warden Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on December 23, 2023. Being [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]], it cost 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 15,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Sentinel Warden Skin can be applied to a [[Warden Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 8 different animated versions.

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Golden Dragon pets.; This skin will allow you to swap between 15 cute animated plushies!; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; ULTIMATE COSMETIC
The Super Plushie Golden Dragon Skin is an [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that can be purchased from [[Taylor|Taylor's Collection]].
## Obtaining
The Super Plushie Golden Dragon Skin can be purchased from [[Taylor]] as part of her permanent collection. Being [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]], it costs 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage
The Super Plushie Golden Dragon Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Golden Dragon Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 15 different animated versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 15 cute animated plushies!

Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TWO_IQ_POINT
Tooltip: Increases your total  Rift Time by 2%.; * Soulbound * X Rift-Transferable X UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
2 IQ Points is a [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]] [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|9x [[IQ Point]]|Recipe: [[IQ Point]] ×9 → 2 IQ Points|
## Usage
Accessory:
2 IQ Points
[Effects]
[Increases your total Rift Time by 2%.]
Accessory:
2 IQ Points (Outside) ([[2 IQ Points]])
[Effects]
[Increases your total Intelligence by 2%.]
The 2 IQ Points' ability in the [[Rift Dimension]] increases total [[Rift Time]] by 2%. Due to being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], it can transferred outside of the [[Rift Dimension]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. Doing so makes it increase [[Intelligence]] by 2%.
## Trivia
- Assuming a 66% win rate at [[Ubik Von Neumann]]'s minigame, it will take an average of 28 trips to [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]] to acquire 18 [[IQ Point]] to craft [[2 IQ Points]] 2 2 IQ Points.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added 2 IQ Points.|

Aggourdian is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item dropped during the [[Harvest Feast]] used to craft the [[Overpriced Drink]] and [[Turbo Gourd]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
Aggourdian has a 0.006% (1 in 18,000 crops) chance to drop when breaking mature [[Pumpkin|Pumpkins]], a 15% chance to drop from a [[Rat]] and a 30% chance to drop from a [[Field Mouse]] while the [[Harvest Feast]] is ongoing and the crop is in season.
[[Overbloom]] is used to increase the drop chance.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Overpriced Drink]]|None|1x [[Glass Bottle]] [[Aggourdian]] 8x Aggourdian 8x [[Designer Coffee Beans]]|Recipe: Aggourdian x8 + [[Designer Coffee Beans]] x8 + [[Glass Bottle]] → [[Overpriced Drink]]|
|[[Turbo Gourd]]|None|[[Aggourdian]] 2x Aggourdian 2x [[Gloomgourd]] 2x [[Polished Pumpkin]] 2x [[Squash]]|Recipe: Aggourdian x2 + [[Gloomgourd]] x2 + [[Squash]] x2 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x2 → [[Turbo Gourd]]|

All Skills Exp Super-Boost is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]]. It grants +20% [[Pets|Pet]] Exp for all [[Skills]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|

# All-in Aloe Fragment
Aliases: All in Aloe Fragment
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[All-in Aloe Fragment]
Type: Fragment
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ALL_IN_ALOE_FRAGMENT
Tooltip: Hope you hit it big!; Right-click to view recipes!; * Co-op Soulbound * UNCOMMON
An All-in Aloe Fragment is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
### All-in Aloe
An All-in Aloe Fragments can be obtained by harvesting the [[All-in Aloe]] mutation. The mutation has 27 Growth Stages, which increase the yield, but also the chance for the mutation to reset to its Growth Stage to 1.
#### Fragment Yield
All-in-Aloe Growth Stages:
|Growth Stage|Drop Multiplier|Chance to Reset|Chance to Reach Stage|1 in #|Expected Stages to Reach|Expected Drops|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|1|0x|0.00%|100.00%|1|0|0|
|2|0x|0.00%|100.00%|1|1|0|
|3|0x|0.00%|100.00%|1|2|0|
|4|1x|3.00%|100.00%|1|3|1|
|5|2x|6.00%|97.00%|1.03|4.12|1.94|
|6|3x|9.00%|91.18%|1.1|5.45|2.74|
|7|4x|12.00%|82.97%|1.21|7.09|3.32|
|8|6x|15.00%|73.02%|1.37|9.19|4.38|
|9|9x|18.00%|62.06%|1.61|11.99|5.59|
|10|13x|21.00%|50.89%|1.96|15.84|6.62|
|11|20x|24.00%|40.21%|2.49|21.32|8.04|
|12|28x|27.00%|30.56%|3.27|29.37|8.56|
|13|41x|30.00%|22.31%|4.48|41.6|9.15|
|14|60x|33.00%|15.61%|6.4|60.85|9.37|
|15|86x|36.00%|10.46%|9.56|92.32|9|
|16|125x|39.00%|6.70%|14.94|145.81|8.37|
|17|182x|42.00%|4.08%|24.48|240.68|7.43|
|18|263x|45.00%|2.37%|42.22|416.69|6.23|
|19|382x|48.00%|1.30%|76.76|759.43|4.98|
|20|554x|51.00%|0.68%|147.61|1462.36|3.75|
|21|803x|54.00%|0.33%|301.24|2986.45|2.67|
|22|1164x|57.00%|0.15%|654.86|6494.47|1.78|
|23|1688x|60.00%|0.07%|1522.93|15105.74|1.11|
|24|2448x|63.00%|0.03%|3807.33|37766.84|0.64|
|25|3549x|66.00%|0.01%|10290.1|102075.25|0.34|
|26|5146x|69.00%|0.00%|30265|300224.27|0.17|
|27|7462x|72.00%|0.00%|97629|968468.62|0.08|
For every [[All-in Aloe Fragment]] 9 All-in Aloe Fragments, the mutation grants 1 [[All-in Aloe]] instead.
Higher stages offer higher raw drops, but lower drops per unit of time due to cumulative reset risk and longer growth duration. On average, it is best to harvest at Growth Stage 14 to maximize drops. However, for completion, Growth Stage 11 is better as it will grant 2 [[All-in Aloe]], enough for analysis and purchasing [[Rose Dragon Pet|Rose Dragon Egg]] from [[Ludleth]]. Realistically, if the intent is purely growing as many aloes as possible to maximize profit then it is recommended to harvest at some point past Growth Stage 14 due to the possibility of missing cycles (via being asleep or busy), as well as reducing cost of replanting mutations used to grow the aloe.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[All-in Aloe]]|None|[[All-in Aloe Fragment]] 9x All-in Aloe Fragment|Recipe: All-in Aloe Fragment ×9 → [[All-in Aloe]]|
## Trivia
- Despite not being a [[Mutations|Mutation]], All-in Aloe Fragment can be stored in the [[Mutations Sack|Mutations Sacks]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added All-in Aloe Fragment.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added All-in Aloe Fragment.|

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Areita Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Battle Arachnid]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Battle Arachnid ([[Attributes]]); Your Spider Pet and Tarantula Pet gain +2–20 [[Crit Damage]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Item ID: ARROW_SWAPPER
Tooltip: Select the arrow type you want to take priority in your quiver.; Selected: None; Left-click: Previous arrow Right-click: Next arrow; UNCOMMON
The Arrow Swapper is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item which cyles through the arrow type the player are using in their [[Quiver]].
## Obtaining
The Arrow Swapper can be purchased from [[Jax]] for 1,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Right-clicking the Arrow Swapper will allow the player to switch between arrows in their quiver, to the right from the current arrow (next). Left-clicking will do the opposite, and switch to arrows to the left in the quiver (previous). The Arrow Swapper defaults to None, meaning no arrows will shoot by default. Without the Arrow Swapper, players can only use flint arrows.

Backwater Armor is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Armor]] and [[Equipment]] set purchased from [[Junker Joel]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing V (5)]] to use.
## Obtaining
Backwater Armor has 4 [[Armor]] pieces and 4 [[Equipment]] pieces, making it an 8 piece set. Each piece can be bought from the [[Junker Joel]] for the respective [[Angler Armor]] piece and 4 [[Rusty Coin]].
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Backwater Helmet ([[Backwater Armor]]); [[Health]]: +30 [[Defense]]: +15 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +2 [[Treasure Chance]]: +0.1|Backwater Chestplate ([[Backwater Armor]]); [[Health]]: +50 [[Defense]]: +25 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +2 [[Treasure Chance]]: +0.1|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Backwater Leggings ([[Backwater Armor]]); [[Health]]: +40 [[Defense]]: +20 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +2 [[Treasure Chance]]: +0.1|Backwater Boots ([[Backwater Armor]]); [[Health]]: +20 [[Defense]]: +10 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +2 [[Treasure Chance]]: +0.1|
|Necklace|Cloak|
|[[Backwater Necklace]]; [[Fishing Speed]]: +2 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +1 [[Treasure Chance]]: +0.1|[[Backwater Cloak]]; [[Fishing Speed]]: +2 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +1 [[Treasure Chance]]: +0.1|
|Belt|Bracelet|
|[[Backwater Belt]]; [[Fishing Speed]]: +2 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +1 [[Treasure Chance]]: +0.1|[[Backwater Gloves]]; [[Fishing Speed]]: +2 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +1 [[Treasure Chance]]: +0.1|
[Total]

[Effects]
[Tiered Bonus: Swamp Soldier; Increases damage dealt to Sea Creatures by +5%-40%.; Pieces Required: 1+]
## Trivia
- The Backwater Armor shares the Color ⁠ #017D31 with [[Rotten Armor|Rotten Chestplate]] and [[Rotten Armor|Rotten Leggings]].
- Upgrading an [[Angler Armor|Angler Helmet]] with the [[Deep Sea Skin]] applied into a Backwater Helmet preserves the skin, even though the skin cannot be applied manually.

The Bait Sack is a [[Sacks|Sack]] unlocked from the [[Raw Cod|Raw Cod III (3)]], [[Raw Cod|Raw Cod V (5)]], and [[Raw Cod|Raw Cod VII (7)]] collections with increasing capacity. The Small Sack can hold 2,240 of each item, the Medium Sack can hold 6,720 of each item, and the Large Sack can hold 20,160 of each item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])|Raw Cod III (3) ([[Raw Cod]])|8x [[Raw Cod]]|Recipe: [[Raw Cod]] ×8 → [[Bait Sack]]|
|Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])|Raw Cod V (5) ([[Raw Cod]])|1x Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]) 8x [[Enchanted Raw Cod]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Raw Cod]] ×8 + [[Bait Sack]] → [[Bait Sack]]|
|Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])|Raw Cod VII (7) ([[Raw Cod]])|1x Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]) 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Raw Cod]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Raw Cod]] x16 ×8 + [[Bait Sack]] → [[Bait Sack]]|
## Usage
The Bait Sack can store [[Bait|Fishing Bait]].
|Sack|Capacity|Items|
|-|-|-|
|Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])|2,240|[[Blessed Bait]], [[Carrot Bait]], [[Corrupted Bait]], [[Dark Bait]], [[Fish Bait]], [[Frozen Bait]], [[Glowy Chum Bait]], [[Golden Bait]], [[Hot Bait]], [[Hotspot Bait]], [[Ice Bait]], [[Light Bait]], [[Minnow Bait]], [[Shark Bait]], [[Spiked Bait]], [[Spooky Bait]], [[Treasure Bait]], [[Whale Bait]], [[Wooden Bait]], [[Worm Bait]], Obfuscated-1 (Bronze) ([[Trophy Fish]]), Obfuscated-1 (Silver) ([[Trophy Fish]]), Obfuscated-1 (Gold) ([[Trophy Fish]]), Obfuscated-1 (Diamond) ([[Trophy Fish]]), Obfuscated-2 (Bronze) ([[Trophy Fish]]), Obfuscated-2 (Silver) ([[Trophy Fish]]), Obfuscated-2 (Gold) ([[Trophy Fish]]), Obfuscated-2 (Diamond) ([[Trophy Fish]])|
|Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])|6,720|(same as above)|
|Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])|20,160|(same as above)|
Baits in the Bait Sack can be interacted with ⇧ Shift+Left Click to be favored as the currently used [[Bait|Fishing Bait]]. Usage of baits from the Bait Sack can be disabled by clicking Use Bait From Sacks button in the sack's inventory.
## Trivia
- The Bait Sack replaced the [[Fishing Bag]].
- The Bait Sack was added by 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] CandyPat as part of the [[Minister Election|Minister Elections]].
  - This minister initially wanted to add a Capybara Pet which could double [[Dyes|Dye]] drops. However that idea didn't work and was changed into the Capybara Sanctuary, which would give blessings that give temporary foraging buffs. These ideas where scrapped and replaced with the Bait Sack.
- The Bait Sack is the only [[Sacks|Sack]] that can be opened directly through commands.
- With a full Large Bait Sack, filleting all 20,160 (315 stacks) [[Trophy Fish|Obfuscated-2 (Diamond)]] would reward the player with 2,419,200 (37,800 stacks) [[Magmafish]], worth .
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Sep 21, 2026|Added Small Bait Sack.|
|Sep 21, 2026|Added Medium Bait Sack.|
|Sep 21, 2026|Added Large Bait Sack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Added Small Bait Sack.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Added Medium Bait Sack.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Added Large Bait Sack.|

Tooltip: Strong Arms I Your Axe gains +3  Sweep on throws.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 5.; UNCOMMON FOREST SHARD (ID U25)
The Bambuleaf Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Strong Arms]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Bambuleaf Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Bambuleaf]].
### Huntraps
A Bambuleaf Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Tranquility Sanctum]] (Spring or Summer).
### Fusion
A Bambuleaf Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Bambuleaf Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Strong Arms]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Strong Arms ([[Attributes]]); Your Axe gains +3–30 [[Sweep]] on throws.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Bambuleaf Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Bambuleaf Shard has the following description:
- This gentle giant is often seen lounging lazily among the bamboo, munching on leaves with a contented expression.

The Bayou Sludge Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Compost Speed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Bayou Sludge Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Bayou Sludge]] with enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
A Bayou Sludge Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Backwater Bayou]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Bayou Sludge Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Bayou Sludge Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Compost Speed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Compost Speed ([[Attributes]]); Your [[Composter]] works +1%–10% faster.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Bayou Sludge Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]

Buy: 150 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]]); 1 [[Bingo Talisman]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BINGO_RING
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; B.I.N.G.O.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
Bingo Ring is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]], purchasable from [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo]].
## Obtaining
Bingo Ring can be purchased from [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] for 1 [[Bingo Talisman]] and 150 [[Bingo|Bingo Points]] once the player has reached Ⓑ Bingo Rank II (2).
## Usage
[[Bingo Artifact]] can be bought from [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] with 1x [[Bingo Ring]] Bingo Ring and 150 [[Bingo|Bingo Points]] once the player has reached Ⓑ Bingo Rank III (3).
## Trivia
- Alongside [[Hegemony Artifact]] and [[Hocus-Pocus Cipher]] (outside of the rift) it does not grant any effects, which means it can only grant boosts through [[Powers|Accessory Powers]].
- The lore of the item is a reference to the children's song BINGO, where the letters of the word are sung to spell out the name of a farmer's dog.
  - Furthermore, when playing the game Bingo, columns are often distinguished by those letters as opposed to A, B, C...
## History

|Nov 29, 2021|Added Bingo Ring.|

Black Coffee is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Brews|Brew]].
## Obtaining
The player can purchase a Black Coffee for 50,000 [[Coins]] from [[Ezekiel]] or [[Grog]].
## Usage
Black Coffee adds +12 [[Speed]] to [[Potions]] with that stat. It can be used in place of an [[Awkward Potion]] in a [[Brewing Stand]] to take effect.
### Brewing
|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] Recipe|
|-|-|
|[[Black Coffee]] 1 Black Coffee; 1-3x Potion ([[Potions]])|Recipe: Black Coffee|
A Black Coffee can be used with the following [[Potions]]:
- [[Adrenaline Potion]]
- [[Agility Potion]]
- [[Rabbit Potion]]

## Obtaining
The Black Sheep Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on November 7, 2020. Being [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 250 purchases.

# Blessed Bait
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Craftable items, Prismarine Crystals Collection, Fishing
[Blessed Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Prismarine Crystals VI (6) ([[Prismarine Crystals]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 42 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 9x [[Gold Ingot]] 1x [[Raw Cod]] 1x [[Prismarine Crystals]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BLESSED_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Increases the chance for Treasure catches to be GREAT or OUTSTANDING by 50%.; UNCOMMON BAIT
Blessed Bait is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Bait]] that increases the player's chance to get a [[Treasure|GREAT CATCH]] or [[Treasure|OUTSTANDING CATCH]] when catching [[Treasure]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Prismarine Crystals VI (6) ([[Prismarine Crystals]])|1x [[Block of Gold]] 1x [[Raw Cod]] 1x [[Prismarine Crystals]]|Recipe: [[Block of Gold]] + [[Raw Cod]] + [[Prismarine Crystals]] → Blessed Bait|
|Prismarine Crystals VI (6) ([[Prismarine Crystals]])|1x [[Raw Cod]] 1x [[Prismarine Crystals]] 9x [[Gold Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Gold Ingot]] x9 + [[Raw Cod]] + [[Prismarine Crystals]] → Blessed Bait|
### Seal's Treat Bag
128x Blessed Bait have a 5.5% (1 in 18) chance to be obtained from a [[Seal's Treat Bag]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Blessed Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Blessed Bait will be consumed to make caught Treasures 50% more likely to be GREAT or OUTSTANDING. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.
## Trivia
- The texture for Blessed Bait used to be a re-colored version of the [[Fish Hat]], which in turn is a reference to the Pokémon Magikarp.
## History

|Mar 12, 2025|Blessed Bait can no longer be caught as treasure.|
|Mar 12, 2025|Changed effect from 15% chance to grant double drops when fishing to +50% chance for treasure catches to be GREAT or OUTSTANDING.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Blessed Bait as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Blood God Sigil
Categories: Uncommon items, Accessories, Craftable items
[Blood God Sigil]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Blood God Crest]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 1,638,400x (25,600 stacks (1,638,400 items total)) [[Potato]] 819,200x (12,800 stacks (819,200 items total)) [[Raw Porkchop]] 1x [[Blood God Crest]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: [[Blood God Crest]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BLOOD_GOD_SIGIL
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; Tracks a counter for each mob killed while worn.; Gain +2 Strength for each digit on the counter. (Max 7); Counter: None yet!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The Blood God Sigil is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is the upgraded version of the [[Blood God Crest]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Blood God Crest]] 32x [[Enchanted Cooked Porkchop]] 64x [[Enchanted Baked Potato]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Cooked Porkchop]] x8 ×4 + [[Enchanted Baked Potato]] x16 ×4 + [[Blood God Crest]] → Blood God Sigil|
## Usage
The Blood God Sigil tracks how many mobs the player has killed while equipped. It grants +2 [[Strength]] for each digit on its kill counter, capped at +14 [[Strength]]. For example, 1–9 kill(s) grant +2 [[Strength]], 10–99 kills grant +4 [[Strength]], and so on. This is a logarithmic relation, since each stat increase requires ten times as many kills. The amount of [[Strength]] that granted matches this formula: Strength=2*⌊log10(Number of Kills)⌋
## Trivia
- The Blood God Sigil was created by 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] MrFaceplate after becoming a [[Minister Election|Minister]] for [[Candidate Aura|Aura]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 23, 2026|Changed Blood God Sigil recipe.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 2, 2025|Added Blood God Sigil.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Crafting recipe now requires 64 (1 stack) [[Enchanted Baked Potato]] and 32 [[Enchanted Cooked Porkchop]] instead of 4 [[Shiny Token]].|

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Bloodbat Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Bartender Discount]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:

As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Item ID: BOTROOT
Tooltip: Dropped rarely from Nether Wart during the Harvest Feast event.; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Botroot is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item dropped during the [[Harvest Feast]] used to craft [[Super Jacob Entertainment System]] and [[Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
Botroot has a 0.006% (1 in 18,000 crops) chance to drop when breaking mature [[Nether Wart]], a 15% chance to drop from a [[Beetle]] and a 30% chance to drop from a [[Field Mouse]] while the [[Harvest Feast]] is ongoing and the crop is in season.
[[Overbloom]] is used to increase the drop chance.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination]]|None|1x [[Jukebox]] 3x [[Cheesebite]] [[Botroot]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Botroot|Recipe: [[Cheesebite]] ×3 + Botroot x16 ×5 + [[Jukebox]] → [[Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination]]|
|[[Super Jacob Entertainment System]]|None|1x [[PlantBoy Advance]] 2x [[Chirping Stereo]] 3x [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 32x [[Enchanted Paper]] [[Botroot]] 64x Botroot|Recipe: [[Chirping Stereo]] ×2 + [[PlantBoy Advance]] + Botroot x32 ×2 + [[Enchanted Paper]] x32 + [[Enchanted Iron Block]] ×3 → [[Super Jacob Entertainment System]]|

The Bramble Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Wood Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Bramble Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] and Forest Category
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Bramble Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Wood Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Wood Elemental ([[Attributes]]); Grants +2–20 [[Health]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Bramble Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Bramble Shard has the following description:
- Bramble elementals have a strong connection to forests, often serving as their guardians.

Tooltip: Health: +50; Gemstones: [⚔]; Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty (0\ 2); Gain immunity to Sea Creatures but they are immune to you and you can no longer catch them.; Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing (0\ 4); Grants +0♔ Trophy Chance.; Full Set Bonus: Star Fisher (0\ 4); Grants +0.2 Fishing Speed per ✪ Upgrade Star on your armor.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Novice Trophy Fisher.; * Soulbound * UNCOMMON HELMET
Bronze Hunter Armor is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Armor]] set used for [[Fishing]].
## Obtaining
Bronze Hunter Armor can be obtained from [[Odger]] after reaching [[Odger|Novice Trophy Fisher]] rank. Once obtained, it can be purchased from Odger's [[Odger|Lost Rewards Shop]] for 100,000 [[Coins]] per piece.
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Bronze Hunter Helmet ([[Bronze Hunter Armor]]); [[Health]]: +50|Bronze Hunter Chestplate ([[Bronze Hunter Armor]]); [[Health]]: +100|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Bronze Hunter Leggings ([[Bronze Hunter Armor]]); [[Health]]: +75|Bronze Hunter Boots ([[Bronze Hunter Armor]]); [[Health]]: +25|
[Total]
[Bronze Hunter Armor Health: +250]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Star Fisher; Grants +0.2Fishing Speed per ✪ Upgrade Star on your armor. Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing; Grants +0 (1 Piece)➜3 (2 Pieces)➜4 (3 Pieces)➜5 (4 Pieces) Trophy Chance. Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty; Gain immunity to Sea Creatures but they are immune to you and you can no longer catch them.; Pieces Required: 2+]
Due to its Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty, players no longer catch [[Sea Creatures]] and cannot do damage to them. However, they also gain immunity to any damage from Sea Creatures. The Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing also increases the player's chances of catching Trophy Fish.

|✪✪✪✪✪|2x [[Diamond Magmafish]]; 5x [[Heavy Pearl]]; 1,000,000 [[Coins]]|3x [[Diamond Magmafish]]; 15x [[Gold Magmafish]]; 12x [[Heavy Pearl]]; 200x [[Magmafish]]; 70x [[Silver Magmafish]]; 1,935,000 [[Coins]]|8x [[Diamond Magmafish]]; 20x [[Heavy Pearl]]; 4,000,000 [[Coins]]|12x [[Diamond Magmafish]]; 60x [[Gold Magmafish]]; 48x [[Heavy Pearl]]; 800x [[Magmafish]]; 280x [[Silver Magmafish]]; 7,740,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- Since Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty and Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing only require 2 pieces to activate, [[Froggles]] and a [[Red Sweater]] can be worn when fishing for [[Trophy Frogs]].
## Trivia
- If players would like to loot share Sea Creatures, they can get the damage requirement and switch to Trophy Hunter Armor in order to not die to them. This is especially noticeable for Boss Sea Creatures, such as [[Thunder]] and [[Lord Jawbus]], since they do a lot of damage.
- Bronze Hunter Armor nullifies the effects of [[Sea Creature Chance]], without affecting the stat itself.

A Bronze Trophy Frog Sack is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Sacks|Sack]] that holds 512 of each Bronze [[Trophy Frogs|Trophy Frog]].
## Obtaining
A Bronze Trophy Frog Sack can be obtained as a reward for reaching [[Researcher Ribery|Novice Frog Collector]]. If lost, it can be bought from from [[Researcher Ribery]] for 50,000 [[Coins]] after reaching the milestone.
## Usage
The Bronze Trophy Frog Sack can store Bronze [[Trophy Frogs]].

# Budget Hopper
Categories: Uncommon items, Minion upgrades, Craftable items, Iron Ingot Collection, Minion Modifiers, Automated shipping items, Hopper
[Budget Hopper]
Type: Minion Upgrade ([[Minion Upgrades]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Iron Ingot V (5) ([[Iron Ingot]])
Function: Sells items for 50.0% of their selling price when the minion is full.
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Enchanted Hopper]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1,200 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 800x (12 stacks plus 32 (800 in total)) [[Iron Ingot]] 8x [[Planks]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BUDGET_HOPPER
Tooltip: Minion Shipping; This item can be placed inside any minion. Automatically sells generated items when the minion has no space.; Items are sold for 50% of their selling price.; UNCOMMON
Budget Hopper is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Automated Shipping]] item for [[Minions]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Iron Ingot V (5) ([[Iron Ingot]])|1x [[Chest]] 5x [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] ×5 + [[Chest]] → Budget Hopper|
## Usage
Budget Hopper can be placed in all [[Minions]]. It makes the minion sell overfill items at 50% of their NPC sell price. The [[Coins]] can be collected by clicking the hopper in the minion's inventory, this will cause the [[Coins]] to be placed in the player's purse.
There is no maximum amount of [[Coins]] that a Budget Hopper can hold, other than the 64-bit float limit of 1.79769e+308 (179769313486231570814527423731704356798070567525844996598917476803157260780028538760589558632766878171540458953514382464234321326889464182768467546703537516986049910576551282076245490090389328944075868508455133942304583236903222948165808559332123348274797826204144723168738177180919299881250404026184124858368).
## Tips
- Collecting coins from an Budget Hopper (or [[Enchanted Hopper]]) will not grant collection or skill experience. For those, the minion will have to periodically be emptied manually, in which case [[Minion Storage|Minion Storages]] and a [[Super Compactor 3000]] or [[Dwarven Super Compactor]] are recommended to have to collect less frequently.
## Trivia
- Under several assumptions (- A Hub island lobby can be loaded forever - The game's tick counter will not overflow - The Enchanted Bread and storage slots can be neglected), round-off error would cause the Budget Hopper of [[Arthur]]'s [[Wheat Minion]] to get stuck at ≈36Qu [[Coins]]. It would take ≈4.87 billion years (assuming 1 year = 31556952 seconds) for the Budget Hopper to reach that point.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Budget Hopper.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Budget Hopper to the [[Bazaar]].|

|[[Suspicious Stew]]|Mycelium II (2) ([[Mycelium]])|1x Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 1x [[Bowl]] [[Burning Eye]] 1x Burning Eye 1x [[Spectre Dust]] 1x [[Mycelium]]|Recipe: [[Mushroom]] + Burning Eye + [[Mycelium]] + [[Spectre Dust]] + [[Bowl]] → [[Suspicious Stew]]|

# Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination
Aliases: Buzzybee, Disco, Disco Destination

Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])

Tooltip: Place this in your Garden and fill it with Pest Vinyls.; Offline  Pests will spawn within 10; blocks of this if their Pest Vinyl has been placed in this.; UNCOMMON
Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Jukebox]] 3x [[Cheesebite]] 80x [[Botroot]]|Recipe: [[Cheesebite]] ×3 + [[Botroot]] x16 ×5 + [[Jukebox]] → Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination|
## Usage
Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination can be placed anywhere in [[The Garden]] and filled with [[Pest Vinyls]]. Offline [[Pests]] will spawn within 10 blocks of it if their corresponding vinyl has been inserted. Only one Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination can be placed on [[The Garden]].
## Trivia
- Despite Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination playing note particles when active, no sound is made.

Tooltip: Dropped rarely from Cactus during the Harvest Feast event.; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Cactus Flower is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item dropped during the [[Harvest Feast]] used to craft the [[Blooming Thorns]], [[Prickly Creeper]], and [[Potted Cactus]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
Cactus Flower has a 0.006% (1 in 18,000 crops) chance to drop when breaking mature [[Cactus]], a 15% chance to drop from a [[Mite]] and a 30% chance to drop from a [[Field Mouse]] while the [[Harvest Feast]] is ongoing and the crop is in season.
[[Overbloom]] is used to increase the drop chance.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Prickly Creeper]]|None|7x [[Enchanted Cactus]] [[Cactus Flower]] 16x Cactus Flower 64x [[Enchanted Gunpowder]]|Recipe: Cactus Flower x16 + [[Enchanted Cactus]] ×7 + [[Enchanted Gunpowder]] x64 → [[Prickly Creeper]]|
|[[Potted Cactus]]|None|1x [[Enchanted Cactus]] 1x [[Enchanted Clay Block]] 1x [[Thornshade]] [[Cactus Flower]] 2x Cactus Flower|Recipe: [[Thornshade]] + Cactus Flower ×2 + [[Enchanted Cactus]] + [[Enchanted Clay Block]] → [[Potted Cactus]] x60|
|[[Blooming Thorns]]|None|2x [[Thornshade]] 4x [[Thorn Fragment]] [[Cactus Flower]] 18x Cactus Flower 18x [[Compacted Wild Rose]]|Recipe: Cactus Flower x6 ×3 + [[Thorn Fragment]] x2 ×2 + [[Thornshade]] x2 + [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x6 ×3 → [[Blooming Thorns]]|

The Carnival Dart Tube is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can only be obtained when the [[Carnival]] event is active.
## Obtaining
The Carnival Dart Tube is given to the player after talking to the [[Carnival Cowboy]] to play [[Carnival|Zombie Shootout]].
## Usage
The Carnival Dart Tube is used to shoot various Zombies that appear in front the player in order to gain Score and [[Carnival Tokens]]. After the minigame is over, the Carnival Dart Tube is removed from the player's inventory.

Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CARNIVAL_SHOVEL
Tooltip: Used to dig up fruits in the Fruit Digging game.; Right-click to cycle Dowsing Modes!; * Soulbound * UNCOMMON SHOVEL
The Carnival Shovel is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Shovels|Shovel]] that can only be obtained when the [[Carnival]] event is active.
## Obtaining
### Carnival
The Carnival Shovel is given to the player after talking to the [[Carnival Pirateman]] to play [[Carnival|Fruit Digging]].
## Usage
The Carnival Shovel is used to dig Sand in order to acquire Fruit which grants Score for which the player will obtain [[Carnival Tokens]]. Unlike other Carnival Items, it also has the ability to douse.
### Dowsing Modes
Dowsing Modes can be cycled through by right-clicking with the shovel in the players hand.
|Type|Function|
|-|-|
|Mines|Tells the player how many mines are adjacent to the mined block.|
|Anchor|Tells the player the location of the lowest scoring fruit, near the mined block.|
|Treasure|Tells the player the highest scoring fruit, near the mined block.|
After the minigame is over, the Carnival Shovel is removed from the player's inventory.
## History

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CARNIVAL_TICKET
Tooltip: Used to play games at The Carnival when Foxy is elected!; It takes a while for Foxy to prepare her Carnivals, but she's always handing out Tickets to ramp up the hype.; * Co-op Soulbound * UNCOMMON
A Carnival Ticket is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item used to play games in the [[Carnival]].
## Obtaining
### Chivalrous Carnival
While [[Foxy]] is in office with her Chivalrous Carnival perk, Carnival Tickets can be obtained the following ways:
- The [[Carnival Leader]] gives [[Carnival Ticket]] 25 Carnival Tickets for free every real-life day.
- [[Chantelle]] will reward [[Carnival Ticket]] 5 Carnival Tickets for each completed Carnival Goal, up to [[Carnival Ticket]] 75 (1 stack plus 11) Carnival Tickets.
### A Time for Giving
- [[Party Chest|Party Chests]] have a 20.703% chance to reward [[Carnival Ticket]] 1 Carnival Ticket, rolled 2-4 times.
Carnival Tickets can also be obtained by the following methods:
### Jacob's Farming Contest
|Bracket|Amount|
|-|-|
|BRONZE|[[Carnival Ticket]] 1 Carnival Ticket|
|SILVER|[[Carnival Ticket]] 1 Carnival Ticket|
|GOLD|[[Carnival Ticket]] 2 Carnival Tickets|
|PLATINUM|[[Carnival Ticket]] 2 Carnival Tickets|
|DIAMOND|[[Carnival Ticket]] 3 Carnival Tickets|
### Garden Visitors
|NPC|Amount|
|-|-|
|[[Chantelle]]|[[Carnival Ticket]] 5 Carnival Ticket|
### Spooky Festival
|Bracket|Amount|
|-|-|
|Wood|[[Carnival Ticket]] 5 Carnival Tickets|
|Stone|[[Carnival Ticket]] 10 Carnival Tickets|
|Iron|[[Carnival Ticket]] 15 Carnival Tickets|
|Gold|[[Carnival Ticket]] 20 Carnival Tickets|
|Diamond|[[Carnival Ticket]] 25 Carnival Tickets|
|Emerald|[[Carnival Ticket]] 35 Carnival Tickets|
|Top 50|[[Carnival Ticket]] 50 Carnival Tickets|
### Harvest Feast
Each [[Harvest Feast|Harvest Feast Milestone]] grants [[Carnival Ticket]] 5 Carnival Ticket.
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv160]|[[Great White Shark]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|
|[Lv99]|[[Tiger Shark]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.25%|
|[Lv52]|[[Blue Shark]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.15%|
|[Lv22]|[[Nurse Shark]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.1%|
## Usage
Carnival Tickets can be used in the [[Carnival]] to play games, in order to earn [[Carnival Tokens]]. [[Carnival Ticket]] 5 Carnival Tickets are consumed upon entering a game.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Events Sack]]|None|1x [[Spooky Sack]] 1x [[Winter Sack]] [[Carnival Ticket]] 70x (1 stack plus 6 (70 in total)) Carnival Ticket|Recipe: Carnival Ticket x10 ×7 + [[Winter Sack]]/[[Spooky Sack]] + [[Spooky Sack]]/[[Winter Sack]] → [[Events Sack]]|
## Trivia
- Carnival Tickets that are in the player's [[Events Sack]] can still be used to play Carnival Games without having to take them out.
- Originally, the text on the description read "when Foxy is Mayor!", at some point, it was changed to "when Foxy is elected!".
## History

|Jun 3, 2024|Added Carnival Ticket.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Added Carnival Ticket.|

Carrot Zest is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item dropped during the [[Harvest Feast]] used to craft [[Poignant Carrot Bait]] and [[Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set|Carrot-Colored Vinyl Sets]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
Carrot Zest has a 0.006% (1 in 18,000 crops) chance to drop when breaking mature [[Carrot|Carrots]], a 15% chance to drop from a [[Cricket]] and a 30% chance to drop from a [[Field Mouse]] while the [[Harvest Feast]] is ongoing and the crop is in-season.
[[Overbloom]] is used to increase the drop chance.
## Usage
### Crafting

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Poignant Carrot Bait]]|None|1x [[Carrot Bait]] [[Carrot Zest]] 32x Carrot Zest|Recipe: Carrot Zest x4 ×8 + [[Carrot Bait]] → [[Poignant Carrot Bait]]|
|[[Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set]]|None|[[Carrot Zest]] 9x Carrot Zest 15x [[Enchanted Oak Log]]|Recipe: Carrot Zest x3 ×3 + [[Enchanted Oak Log]] x3 ×5 → [[Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set]]|

# Cat Talisman

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CAT_TALISMAN
Tooltip: Speed: +1; Accessory Power: +5; * Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The Cat Talisman is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that grants the player +1 [[Speed]].
## Obtaining
The Cat Talisman is obtained through the [[Dungeon Hub]] by completing 4/4 of any of the [[Dungeon Hub Race|Dungeon Races]]. Races can be started by talking to [[Guildford]] next to the start race pad.
If the player loses the item, it can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Cat Talisman [[Speed]]: +1
## Upgrading
The Cat Talisman can be upgraded twice from completing 4/4 in any other races, the other races won't give anything for completing the 4th one. It upgrades to the [[Lynx Talisman]] at 2 4/4's then to the [[Cheetah Talisman]] at 3 4/4s.
For example, completing Crystal Core No Abilities and Precursor Ruins No Abilities will award the Lynx Talisman. However, completing Precursor Ruins No Abilities and Precursor Ruins Grappling Hooks will only award the Cat Talisman.
(4/4 means the player has to beat a single race 4 times, with decreasing time limits.)
## Trivia
- Its texture looks almost identical to the [[Black Cat Pet]], except it has more teal eyes compared to the mostly blue eyes of the Black Cat Pet.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Cat Talisman.|
|Apr 25, 2022|Cat Talisman was added to [[Rusty]]'s shop and is now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Fixed Cat Talisman typo.|
|Nov 28, 2023|0.19.8|Changed from Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]) to [[Soulbound]].|

## Usage
Wand:
Celeste Wand
[Effects]
[Ability: Lightning Strike RIGHT CLICK; Strikes lightning up to 10 blocks away, dealing 250 damage to the nearest monster.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 25; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.3]
Right clicking with the Celeste Wand unleashes a lightning bolt on the location of the caster's crosshair up to 10 blocks away. [[Mob|Mobs]] struck take [[Ability Damage]]. It costs 25 [[Mana]] to use and has no cooldown.

The Cheeky Tiki Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Bayou Treasure]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Cheeky Tiki Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Cheeky Tiki]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]].
### Fusion
A Cheeky Tiki Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Cheeky Tiki Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Bayou Treasure]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Bayou Treasure ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.1–1 [[Treasure Chance]] while in the [[Backwater Bayou]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Cheeky Tiki Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Cheeky Tiki Shard has the following description:

# Chocoberry
Aliases: Choco Berry, Choco berry
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[Chocoberry]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CHOCOBERRY
Tooltip: It looks incredibly tasty, taking a small bite wouldn't hurt the plant, right? Right?; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; UNCOMMON MUTATION
A Chocoberry is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Chocoberry can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 2 [[Gloomgourd]] and 6 [[Choconut]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Chocoberry needs water and has 6 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it retains watering status by 50%.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Chocoberries also drop 440 (6 stacks plus 56) [[Cocoa Beans]], 187 (2 stacks plus 59) [[Pumpkin]], 1,760 (27 stacks plus 32) [[Melon Slice]], and +3,520 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Chocoberry can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 60,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 30 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Chocoberry grants an additional 500 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Chocoberries are used to create a [[Blastberry]].
### Abiphone Contact
A Chocoberry can be given to [[Jake]] in exchange for their [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])|DNA Analysis Milestone III (3) ([[Crop Analyzer]])|1x Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]) 4x [[Coalroot]] 4x [[Creambloom]] [[Chocoberry]] 8x Chocoberry|Recipe: Chocoberry x2 ×4 + [[Creambloom]] x2 ×2 + [[Coalroot]] x2 ×2 + [[Mutations Sack]] → [[Mutations Sack]]|
## Gallery
- Chocoberry in the Greenhouse Chocoberry in the [[Greenhouse]]

[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Chum
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Chum]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]]); Medium Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]]); Large Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 5 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CHUM
Tooltip: Obtained from killing a sea creature with a nearby Chum Bucket.; Deposit it in any Chum Bucket to earn +20 Coins and +10 Fishing XP!; UNCOMMON
Chum is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Chum can be obtained by killing [[Sea Creatures]] near a placed [[Chum Bucket]]. Killed Sea Creatures drop [[Chum]] 1 Chum, which is increased by 1 per additional nearby Chum Bucket, up to 4. The amount can also be increased by 2 with [[Glowy Chum Bait]].
Chum can also be obtained by catching items or Sea Creatures with a [[Chum Sinker]] applied to any fishing rod.
[[Attributes|Bucket Lover]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants up to 50% chance to get additional Chum with when killing [[Sea Creatures]].
Accepting [[Moby]]'s [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]] request will grant 1 Chum.
## Usage
### Filling Chum Buckets
Right-clicking on any placed [[Chum Bucket|Empty Chum Bucket]] while holding Chum will deposit the Chum into the Chum Bucket. Up to 10 Chum can be put in a Chum Bucket. Each Chum gives 20 [[Coins]] and +10 [[Fishing|Fishing XP]] when put in a Chum Bucket. Using the [[Luminous Bracelet]] grants an extra 20 [[Coins]] and +10 [[Fishing|Fishing XP]] when depositing Chum into somebody else's Chum Bucket.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]])|None|1x Empty Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]]) [[Chum]] 10x Chum|Recipe: Chum x10 + [[Chum Bucket]] → [[Chum Bucket]]|
|Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]])|None|1x [[Empty Chumcap Bucket]] [[Chum]] 10x Chum|Recipe: Chum x10 + [[Empty Chumcap Bucket]] → [[Chum Bucket]]|
## Notes
- The [[Fishing]] XP gain is not affected by [[Fishing Wisdom]].
- The [[Chum Sinker]] replaced the [[Chum Rod]], the latter no longer being obtainable other than trading.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 6, 2022|Added Chum.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Chum to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Unknown Date|Players can now deposit Chum in the Chum Bucket of ♲ Ironman ([[Ironman]]) players.|

Tooltip: A hazy flower that glows like dying embers, stuck somewhere between fire and life.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; UNCOMMON MUTATION
A Cindershade is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Cindershade can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Ashwreath]] and 4 [[Witherbloom]] within 1 block of [[Soul Sand]].
While growing, a Cindershade does not need water and has 8 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it increases yield by 30%, spreads buffs and debuffs to adjacent crops, and reduces [[Farming]] experience by -20%.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Cindershades also drop 1,020 (15 stacks plus 60) [[Nether Wart]], 680 (10 stacks plus 40) [[Wild Rose]], and +2,720 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Cindershade can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 80,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 40 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Cindershade grants an additional 500 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Cindershades are used to create a [[Fleshtrap]] and [[Zombud]].

- Cindershade in the Greenhouse Cindershade in the [[Greenhouse]]

The Cleaver is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Swords|Sword]] unlocked at [[Gold Ingot|Gold Ingot II (2)]]. This weapon should not be confused with the [[Cleave]] enchantment, which applies the Cleaver's ability to any sword, or the [[Super Cleaver]] and [[Hyper Cleaver]], which, while similar, are dungeon items and are much stronger. Due to its relatively low stats, it's generally not useful except in very Early Game.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Gold Ingot II (2) ([[Gold Ingot]])|2x [[Stick]] 4x [[Gold Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Gold Ingot]] ×4 + [[Stick]] ×2 → Cleaver|
## Usage
Sword:
Cleaver [[Damage]]: +40 [[Strength]]: +10
[Effects]
[Ability: Cleave; When hitting an entity, monsters in a 3 block range will be hit for a portion of that damage too.]
The damage on adjacent mobs typically ranges from 40% to 50% of the damage dealt to the first mob. However, as the distance increases the damage decreases. Critical strikes do increase the damage dealt to adjacent mobs.
The item ability adds a hit tick to nearby mobs, making it take a bit longer to deal damage to them again.
### Merchants
A Cleaver alongside 80,000 [[Coins]] is required to purchase a [[Super Cleaver]] from [[Ophelia]].
## Trivia
- The ability, Cleave, is likely inspired from the item ability Cleave from the game League of Legends.
